Civil Engineering & Interior Architecture

Best Interior Designer in Birgunj, Nepal

If you are looking for the best interior designer, I am Chetan Kumar Sarraf, a qualified Civil Engineer & Interior Design Professional. Integrating structural civil engineering accuracy with 3D photorealism, spatial BOQ valuation, and technical drafting, I provide premium interior design services tailored for residential and commercial spaces across Birgunj, Parsa, and Nepal.

Er. Chetan Kr. Sarraf
Civil Engineer
Birgunj, Parsa, Nepal
02. Spatial Philosophy

Architecture as a Living Narrative

We believe architecture should never be treated as static decoration, but as an ongoing dialogue between human living, structural honesty, and local Terai context.

01

Narrative as Spatial Flow

Every threshold, natural light well, and ceiling recess is orchestrated around how the dweller interacts with space.

02

Civil Engineering Rigor

Spatial elegance rests on structural truth. Every load-bearing wall removal and ceiling drop complies with NBC 205:2024.

03

Terai Material Sincerity

Designing for Birgunj requires moisture-resistant HDHMR, anti-termite joinery, and breathable masonry.

04

Quantified Cost Integrity

Our itemized Bill of Quantities (BOQ) prevents arbitrary contractor variations and guarantees precision budgeting.

10. Profile & Qualifications

Where Engineering Meets Rigor Harmonizes with Interior Luxury

Er. Chetan Kumar Sarraf
Nepali (Bilingual) · Bhojpuri (Bilingual) · Hindi (Professional) · English (Professional)
View Official Resume (PDF)

Recognized by homeowners seeking the best interior designer in Birgunj, Nepal, I am a diversified professional who integrates structural Civil Engineering expertise with sophisticated interior architecture and 3D visualization.

Holding an academic background in Civil Engineering from Tri-juddha Secondary School, Birgunj, I specialize in preparing constructible technical blueprints, conducting accurate Bill of Quantities (BOQ) cost controls, and delivering immersive architectural visualizations that balance the client’s vision with structural integrity and budget.
